## Corpus Check
- 2930 files · ~2,206,692 words

## Summary
- 22169 nodes · 38666 edges · 1915 communities (1492 shown, 423 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 99% EXTRACTED · 1% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 274 edges (avg confidence: 0.85)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `32261733`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
